Each call already carries its own known args, so these are independent by construction — fold them into ONE code:run: Promise.all over the inputs, ready-to-paste skeleton, `inputs` prefilled from this burst (extend it instead of firing another solo call). If a NEXT call's args must come from one of these results, sequence just that one separately instead of adding it to this list:\ncode:run { script: `\n  const inputs = [\n    {\"items\":[{\"to\":[\"su-fa4c1948-0cb1-4552-a7a3-dc274c5e1b0a\"],\"expects\":\"action\",\"wake\":\"required\",\"summary\":\"Resume WI-10005145 with a concrete next-action recom /* …truncated — fill in the rest */,\n    {\"items\":[{\"to\":[\"su-80791f26-9327-4ac8-9122-835a25d09896\"],\"expects\":\"action\",\"wake\":\"required\",\"summary\":\"Resume EI-25447581346809844 with recommended next ac /* …truncated — fill in the rest */,\n    /* …the NEXT coord:send args go here — extend this list, not another solo call */\n  ];\n  const out = await Promise.all(\n    inputs.map((a) => tools.coord.send(a).catch((e) => ({ error: String(e) }))),\n  );\n  return out;\n` }\nOnly the script's return re-enters context, so return what you'll need next. Or reuse a recipe (recipes:search { query }). Pass relayOf/relayQuote on THIS send, not after — a text claim (\"owner said/directive/approved\") with neither ships anyway but renders [UNVERIFIED authority claim] to every recipient; re-sending backed does not un-flag the first copy, so a fleet-wide broadcast lands twice.; coord:handoff (transfer WORK, not just a message); coord:dispatch (assign a named work-item and wake its execution owner); coord:inbox (read replies to what you sent); coord:roster { view:\"live\" } (confirm a recipient is awake before wake:required); EI-9940: self-resolved a failure you already reported? reply via related_msg_id, not a fresh send; WI-5341: replying to a message whose `expects` was not `none`? set related_msg_id to it — a broadcast or unthreaded reply keeps you reading as \"unanswered\" in leader-brief/fleet:assignments even though you replied."},{"type":"text","text":"\n[batch-hint] you've called \"coord:send\" from 2× separate turns in the last ~90s — that's 2 separate round-trips, each re-reading your whole context. Each call already carries its own known args, so these are independent by construction — fold them into ONE code:run: Promise.all over the inputs, ready-to-paste skeleton, `inputs` prefilled from this burst (extend it instead of firing another solo call). If a NEXT call's args must come from one of these results, sequence just that one separately instead of adding it to this list:\ncode:run { script: `\n  const inputs = [\n    {\"items\":[{\"to\":[\"su-1020321d-4f7c-4257-a245-c2461a585b62\"],\"expects\":\"action\",\"wake\":\"required\",\"summary\":\"Resume EI-25223159069515108 with recommended next ac /* …truncated — fill in the rest */,\n    {\"items\":[{\"to\":[\"su-16da39cf-6e87-45f1-9c6f-7fa8915f021d\"],\"expects\":\"action\",\"wake\":\"required\",\"summary\":\"Resume WI-6554700010008657 with recommended next act /* …truncated — fill in the rest */,\n    /* …the NEXT coord:send args go here — extend this list, not another solo call */\n  ];\n  const out = await Promise.all(\n    inputs.map((a) => tools.coord.send(a).catch((e) => ({ error: String(e) }))),\n  );\n  return out;\n` }\nOnly the script's return re-enters context, so return what you'll need next. Or reuse a recipe (recipes:search { query }). think)."}]}},{"index":8,"owners":["su-7c708cfc-9f17-43a2-95b7-3a16339fe7a6","su-7ca9a10b-e494-4e71-b3d5-723e257a77d1","su-82592767-2742-45b8-b053-4b096877bacc","su-e5f812b6-2f01-4cb8-8d23-3299e65fc494","su-3e584d48-38e4-43ef-ad0f-8092abf58687"],"result":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"{\"ok\":true,\"results\":[{\"ok\":true,\"to\":[\"su-7c708cfc-9f17-43a2-95b7-3a16339fe7a6\"],\"msg_id\":\"coord-send-b6fadd0d2437c5a50e69752b405b2d33beb6688e1c94ba4b0ada552147227e41\",\"ts\":\"2026-10-12T18:28:58.432Z\",\"federated\":false,\"wake\":{\"mode\":\"required\",\"targets\":[\"su-7c708cfc-9f17-43a2-95b7-3a16339fe7a6\"],\"queued\":1,\"woken\":1,\"pickupConfirmed\":false,\"wakeOutcome\":\"queued\"},\"bodyTruncated\":false,\"bodyMayBeTruncated\":true,\"bodyAuthoredChars\":523,\"bodyFullChars\":523,\"bodyDeliveredChars\":523,\"bodyDeliveryCap\":600,\"bodyCrowdedPageCap\":300,\"bodyReadRef\":{\"tool\":\"coord:read\",\"msg_id\":\"coord-send-b6fadd0d2437c5a50e69752b405b2d33beb6688e1c94ba4b0ada552147227e41\"}},{\"ok\":true,\"to\":[\"su-7ca9a10b-e494-4e71-b3d5-723e257a77d1\"],\"msg_id\":\"coord-send-b4409ed783ae8986f80af3319e80cd86ce066e8fd1fd8dc8865b98b2ae51b7fd\",\"ts\":\"2026-10-12T18:28:58.741Z\",\"federated\":false,\"wake\":{\"mode\":\"required\",\"targets\":[\"su-7ca9a10b-e494-4e71-b3d5-723e257a77d1\"],\"queued\":1,\"woken\":1,\"pickupConfirmed\":false,\"wakeOutcome\":\"queued\"},\"bodyTruncated\":false,\"bodyMayBeTruncated\":true,\"bodyAuthoredChars\":561,\"bodyFullChars\":561,\"bodyDeliveredChars\":561,\"bodyDeliveryCap\":600,\"bodyCrowdedPageCap\":300,\"bodyReadRef\":{\"tool\":\"coord:read\",\"msg_id\":\"coord-send-b4409ed783ae8986f80af3319e80cd86ce066e8fd1fd8dc8865b98b2ae51b7fd\"}},{\"ok\":true,\"to\":[\"su-82592767-2742-45b8-b053-4b096877bacc\"],\"msg_id\":\"coord-send-456bb67dce152cf10800c17a402b3b8e07963045598c14f0ae7355b966757ace\",\"ts\":\"2026-10-12T18:28:59.076Z\",\"federated\":false,\"wake\":{\"mode\":\"required\",\"targets\":[\"su-82592767-2742-45b8-b053-4b096877ba Each call already carries its own known args, so these are independent by construction — fold them into ONE code:run: Promise.all over the inputs, ready-to-paste skeleton, `inputs` prefilled from this burst (extend it instead of firing another solo call). If a NEXT call's args must come from one of these results, sequence just that one separately instead of adding it to this list:\ncode:run { script: `\n  const inputs = [\n    {\"items\":[{\"to\":[\"su-7c708cfc-9f17-43a2-95b7-3a16339fe7a6\"],\"expects\":\"action\",\"wake\":\"required\",\"summary\":\"Resume WI-6554700010008904 with recommended next act /* …truncated — fill in the rest */,\n    {\"items\":[{\"to\":[\"su-cdc1b6ee-4254-4b72-a03d-325374263be7\"],\"expects\":\"action\",\"wake\":\"required\",\"summary\":\"Resume EI-25439753850177762 with recommended next ac /* …truncated — fill in the rest */,\n    /* …the NEXT coord:send args go here — extend this list, not another solo call */\n  ];\n  const out = await Promise.all(\n    inputs.map((a) => tools.coord.send(a).catch((e) => ({ error: String(e) }))),\n  );\n  return out;\n` }\nOnly the script's return re-enters context, so return what you'll need next. Or reuse a recipe (recipes:search { query }). Pass relayOf/relayQuote on THIS send, not after — a text claim (\"owner said/directive/approved\") with neither ships anyway but renders [UNVERIFIED authority claim] to every recipient; re-sending backed does not un-flag the first copy, so a fleet-wide broadcast lands twice.; coord:handoff (transfer WORK, not just a message); coord:dispatch (assign a named work-item and wake its execution owner); coord:inbox (read replies to what you sent); coord:roster { view:\"live\" } (confirm a recipient is awake before wake:required); EI-9940: self-resolved a failure you already reported? reply via related_msg_id, not a fresh send; WI-5341: replying to a message whose `expects` was not `none`? set related_msg_id to it — a broadcast or unthreaded reply keeps you reading as \"unanswered\" in leader-brief/fleet:assignments even though you replied."},{"type":"text","text":"\n[batch-hint] you've called \"coord:send\" from 2× separate turns in the last ~90s — that's 2 separate round-trips, each re-reading your whole context. Each call already carries its own known args, so these are independent by construction — fold them into ONE code:run: Promise.all over the inputs, ready-to-paste skeleton, `inputs` prefilled from this burst (extend it instead of firing another solo call). If a NEXT call's args must come from one of these results, sequence just that one separately instead of adding it to this list:\ncode:run { script: `\n  const inputs = [\n    {\"items\":[{\"to\":[\"su-d66bcdf7-a0e2-46d9-98d0-a2d202bbff54\"],\"expects\":\"action\",\"wake\":\"required\",\"summary\":\"Resume EI-25757313053308974 with recommended next ac /* …truncated — fill in the rest */,\n    {\"items\":[{\"to\":[\"su-de5729a7-76d6-4498-8766-c084b2502a21\"],\"expects\":\"action\",\"wake\":\"required\",\"summary\":\"Resume EI-25420411002163456 with recommended next ac /* …truncated — fill in the rest */,\n    /* …the NEXT coord:send args go here — extend this list, not another solo call */\n  ];\n  const out = await Promise.all(\n    inputs.map((a) => tools.coord.send(a).catch((e) => ({ error: String(e) }))),\n  );\n  return out;\n` }\nOnly the script's return re-enters context, so return what you'll need next. Or reuse a recipe (recipes:search { query }). think)."}]}},{"index":14,"owners":["su-faee16ea-3b47-454a-ae48-f949b51c6d8d","su-f835b8a8-dc40-4cfa-95fa-34ae14a18895","su-25cbe9c5-330e-480b-8946-340ade7e9d0e","su-319d25f7-f3ab-48aa-a152-27b5388ce332","su-e6d2a824-e413-4321-9aea-6600c71cec71"],"result":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"{\"ok\":true,\"results\":[{\"ok\":true,\"to\":[\"su-faee16ea-3b47-454a-ae48-f949b51c6d8d\"],\"msg_id\":\"coord-send-dc8263386e88201d1ba9030236d67cb7fb77f352b15c1ede19e77f63cf3ea22a\",\"ts\":\"2026-10-12T18:30:30.537Z\",\"federated\":false,\"wake\":{\"mode\":\"required\",\"targets\":[\"su-faee16ea-3b47-454a-ae48-f949b51c6d8d\"],\"queued\":1,\"woken\":1,\"pickupConfirmed\":false,\"wakeOutcome\":\"queued\"},\"bodyTruncated\":false,\"bodyMayBeTruncated\":true,\"bodyAuthoredChars\":552,\"bodyFullChars\":552,\"bodyDeliveredChars\":552,\"bodyDeliveryCap\":600,\"bodyCrowdedPageCap\":300,\"bodyReadRef\":{\"tool\":\"coord:read\",\"msg_id\":\"coord-send-dc8263386e88201d1ba9030236d67cb7fb77f352b15c1ede19e77f63cf3ea22a\"}},{\"ok\":true,\"to\":[\"su-f835b8a8-dc40-4cfa-95fa-34ae14a18895\"],\"msg_id\":\"coord-send-0069017ea7476befb201c8e10438553acd742f9aaf544555f989fbad0f471330\",\"ts\":\"2026-10-12T18:30:31.008Z\",\"federated\":false,\"wake\":{\"mode\":\"required\",\"targets\":[\"su-f835b8a8-dc40-4cfa-95fa-34ae14a18895\"],\"queued\":1,\"woken\":1,\"pickupConfirmed\":false,\"wakeOutcome\":\"queued\"},\"bodyTruncated\":false,\"bodyMayBeTruncated\":true,\"bodyAuthoredChars\":554,\"bodyFullChars\":554,\"bodyDeliveredChars\":554,\"bodyDeliveryCap\":600,\"bodyCrowdedPageCap\":300,\"bodyReadRef\":{\"tool\":\"coord:read\",\"msg_id\":\"coord-send-0069017ea7476befb201c8e10438553acd742f9aaf544555f989fbad0f471330\"}},{\"ok\":true,\"to\":[\"su-25cbe9c5-330e-480b-8946-340ade7e9d0e\"],\"msg_id\":\"coord-send-fe5ccbb044e693fc079724242cfbe415c5b797bba723378928a14a13875234ae\",\"ts\":\"2026-10-12T18:30:31.393Z\",\"federated\":false,\"wake\":{\"mode\":\"required\",\"targets\":[\"su-25cbe9c5-330e-480b-8946-340ade7e9 Or reuse a recipe (recipes:search { query }). Please bundle the rest of this burst into a single code:run. you've called \"coord:send\" from 2× separate turns in the last ~90s — that's 2 separate round-trips, each re-reading your whole context. Each call already carries its own known args, so these are independent by construction — fold them into ONE code:run: Promise.all over the inputs, ready-to-paste skeleton, `inputs` prefilled from this burst (extend it instead of firing another solo call). If a NEXT call's args must come from one of these results, sequence just that one separately instead of adding it to this list:\ncode:run { script: `\n  const inputs = [\n    {\"items\":[{\"to\":[\"su-ad62b0d6-4ed9-4075-bc9d-ca28ba758113\"],\"expects\":\"action\",\"wake\":\"required\",\"summary\":\"Automatic account selection restored; resume the exi /* …truncated — fill in the rest */,\n    {\"items\":[{\"to\":[\"su-768e011d-ed5e-4861-baf0-a6ee1be282a8\"],\"expects\":\"action\",\"wake\":\"required\",\"summary\":\"Automatic account selection restored; resume the exi /* …truncated — fill in the rest */,\n    /* …the NEXT coord:send args go here — extend this list, not another solo call */\n  ];\n  const out = await Promise.all(\n    inputs.map((a) => tools.coord.send(a).catch((e) => ({ error: String(e) }))),\n  );\n  return out;\n` }\nOnly the script's return re-enters context, so return what you'll need next. also: EI-21927749510698309: relaying an owner or fleet-leader directive? Pass relayOf/relayQuote on THIS send, not after — a text claim (\"owner said/directive/approved\") with neither ships anyway but renders [UNVERIFIED authority claim] to every recipient; re-sending backed does not un-flag the first copy, so a fleet-wide broadcast lands twice.; coord:handoff (transfer WORK, not just a message); coord:dispatch (assign a named work-item and wake its execution owner); coord:inbox (read replies to what you sent); coord:roster { view:\"live\" } (confirm a recipient is awake before wake:required); EI-9940: self-resolved a failure you already reported? reply via related_msg_id, not a fresh send; WI-5341: replying to a message whose `expects` was not `none`? set related_msg_id to it — a broadcast or unthreaded reply keeps you reading as \"unanswered\" in leader-brief/fleet:assignments even though you replied."}]}}]}
